id=\"offerings_update_form_6918_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eDear Investors,\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e2021 was another year of unexpected challenges in the Consumer Packaged Good industry, and Salsa God was able to successfully navigate the relentless cost pressures that have invaded every aspect of the company's business. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e2021 - Another Year in the Books!\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eGross Revenue\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn 2021, Salsa God generated approximately $430,000 in Gross Revenue, which was flat or slightly above Gross Revenue in 2020. \u0026nbsp;While the company's results did not meet the expectations of internal management, there were a number of positive signs. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eAustralian Market\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eIn 2021, Salsa God shipped its first full 20' container to Real Foods Australia (Salsa God's sole distributor in Australia) generating approximately $30,000 in Gross Revenue. \u0026nbsp;The company first began exporting to Australia in 2018 and has gradually grown its sales sufficiently to begin shipping full container loads to the land down under!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eSprouts Farmers Market\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSalsa God launched nationally into Sprouts Farmers Market in April of 2021, and Sprouts has quickly grown to be Salsa God's largest account by number of stores (over 350 stores across the U.S.). \u0026nbsp;We are very excited to be a valued partner of Sprouts, and we expect to continue to grow our sales within the premium natural retailer. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eWorld Market\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eWorld Market represents Salsa God's first foray into the non-traditional retailer market in the U.S., and it also represents Salsa God's first account using a 12oz jar rather than the traditional 16oz jar. \u0026nbsp;World Market will allow Salsa God to expand its reach to consumers who may not otherwise see Salsa God products on the local grocery store shelves. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e2022 - The Year Ahead\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e2022 holds some very promising news for Salsa God. \u0026nbsp;Our first and biggest news item is also something of a re-release. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eChips \u0026amp; Salsa Combo Pack is Back!\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSalsa God is re-releasing its Chips \u0026amp; Salsa Combo Pack. \u0026nbsp;The company originally released the Chips \u0026amp; Salsa Combo Pack in 2019, but after only a short time in the market, the company's salsa cup co-packer decided to no longer produce salsa cups for Salsa God. \u0026nbsp;This was very difficult news for us to hear, but after 2+ years of searching for another salsa cup co-packer, we have finally found a co-packer who can produce Salsa God's salsa cups again! \u0026nbsp;To further complicate matters, Salsa God's tortilla chip co-packer informed the company that they would not be able to fill our expected demand of tortilla chips due to labor issues, so Salsa God was forced to find another co-packer for our tortilla chips. \u0026nbsp;Plus, we had to do all of this during the COVID lockdown of 2020, which made everything take much longer than expected. \u0026nbsp;But alas, we have our Combo Packs ready to Rock!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.com/p/images/attachments/original/000/060/454/60454-1646251807-fe9996f259d18927c458506f7db400dd2bbc47da.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eCOMBO PACK 2.0 -\u0026nbsp;\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eOur new, soon-to-be re-released Chips \u0026amp; Salsa Combo Pack is an upgrade to our previous version, and we are very excited about this product. \u0026nbsp;We have reduced the size of our Combo Pack from 7oz (4oz salsa cup and 3oz bag of tortilla chips) to 5oz (3oz salsa cup and 2oz bag of tortilla chips). \u0026nbsp;Not only is this a more appropriate portion size for a single-serve item, but it also comes in a smaller box that will more easily fit on to store shelves. \u0026nbsp;We expect our Combo Pack to be a big hit in C-Stores, Airports and even Airlines. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eNatural Products Expo West\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSalsa God is very excited to be exhibiting at the Natural Products Expo West in Anaheim, California from March 9th - March 11th. \u0026nbsp;We hope to generate tons of buzz around our Combo Packs!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eSweets and Snacks Expo\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSalsa God is very excited to be exhibiting at the Sweets \u0026amp; Snacks Expo in Chicago, Illinois from May 23rd - May 26th. \u0026nbsp;We hope to generate tons of buzz in the C-Store channel around our Combo Packs!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eBIG ANNOUNCEMENT\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eFor the past 6 months or so, we have been in discussions with a very large account regarding a partnership. \u0026nbsp;Unfortunately, at the time of this post, we are not yet in a position to definitively say that the partnership will move forward, but we are very optimistic that we can get to a point in the near future to allow us to reveal the big announcement to our investors. \u0026nbsp;Trust us - this is a company-changing partnership, and we have been working night and day to get the deal done. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eInvestor Perks\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSome investors have pointed out that they have not yet received one of the perks from investing $1,000 or more in the company. \u0026nbsp;The perk was that each investor who met the investment threshold was to receive samples of all new products released in 2020. \u0026nbsp;Unfortunately, due to COVID, Salsa God did not release any new products in 2020. \u0026nbsp;However, we are not going to let this deter us from rewarding our investors (U.S. continuous 48 states only). \u0026nbsp;Once the Chips \u0026amp; Salsa Combo Pack is released (expected June 1, 2022), we will be giving each qualified investor a code for a free 3 pack on Amazon. \u0026nbsp;Stay tuned for details, and please ensure that you leave feedback on Amazon to help us grow our sales. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eConclusion\u003c/em\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e2021 did not meet our expectations for growth, but we are excited about the opportunities present in 2022. \u0026nbsp;We hope to grow the business significantly with the relaunch of the Combo Pack, and we truly hope to be able to share the BIG ANNOUNCEMENT with our investors and the world shortly.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eBest wishes for a great 2022 and HAPPY TEXAS INDEPENDENCE DAY!!!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eDanny Mayans\u003cbr\u003eChief Amigo, Salsa God\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1 s-paddingHoriz1 s-borderTop1 u-displayFlex u-alignItemsCenter id=\"offerings_update_form_3263_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eHello Investors,\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eI appreciate your patience, and I am happy to announce Sales Figures for 2020 through the end of Q3. \u0026nbsp;Salsa God saw double digit growth across 4 out of 5 of our major accounts, and even during the pandemic, we were able to eke out a slight revenue gain compared to the first three quarters of 2019. \u0026nbsp;Sales were essentially flat at $311.5k in 2020 vs $310.9k in 2019. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/027/478/27478-1604513652-02a94608ffb8ac0da1f218b5ff3e5caadc0e54c2.png\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAs the chart above shows, four out of five of Salsa God's major accounts experienced revenue growth through Q3 of 2020 vs 2019. \u0026nbsp;The one notable decline was HEB; however the decline is not as worrisome as it would look on the chart. \u0026nbsp;HEB opened a new distribution warehouse in 2020, and in order to ease the transition, HEB placed approximately $75k worth of orders that were delivered in the last two weeks of December 2019. \u0026nbsp;This large order accelerated sales that would have occurred in 2020 into 2019. \u0026nbsp;Normalizing for this by moving the large order in Dec 2019 to 2020's sales figures and HEB sales grew from $94.7k in 2019 to $104.1k in 2020, or a growth rate of 9.9%. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThe chart below shows the percentage growth in sales in Salsa God's major accounts and overall Gross Revenue. \u0026nbsp;Please note that the HEB Growth Rate and the Total Growth Rate have been normalized by moving the $75k of late revenue from 2019 into 2020. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg recent news that we are excited about \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eSalsa God has now launched into Fresh Thyme, a chain of about 73 natural grocery stores located across the Midwest. particularly excited about the Combo Pack's potential to supercharge revenue growth. \u0026nbsp;We believe that the Combo Pack will begin to generate more than $1mm in annual revenue by 2021. \u0026nbsp;\u003cbr\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eNew Product Launches\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003cem\u003eSriracha Salsa\u003c/em\u003e - One day while I was walking through a Whole Foods Market, I noticed that there were about a dozen different brands that were selling Sriracha Hot Sauce. \u0026nbsp;Seeing this made me wonder if there were any Sriracha flavored Salsas, and I found out that there aren't any! \u0026nbsp;So, I set out to make a Sriracha Salsa, and it has become my favorite salsa. \u0026nbsp;We are confident that our Sriracha Salsa is going to take the U.S. by storm. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eGrab \u0026amp; Go Chips \u0026amp; Salsa Combo Pack -\u0026nbsp;\u003c/em\u003eThis product took more than one year to get to the market from conception. \u0026nbsp;I still can't believe that no other company has figured out how to do a single-serve, Grab \u0026amp; Go Chips \u0026amp; Salsa Combo Pack. \u0026nbsp;I can't begin to describe the level of interest that convenience stores, hotels, airports, airlines, grocery stores and other retailers have expressed in carrying this product. \u0026nbsp;I can't believe it myself. \u0026nbsp;Even with all of the interest in the marketplace, Salsa God is not standing still. \u0026nbsp;We will soon be launching two new flavors of our Combo Pack. \u0026nbsp;Hot Heat Salsa and our soon-to-be famous Sriracha Salsa Combo Pack! \u0026nbsp;We expect HUGE results here.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003e\u003cu\u003eChannel Expansion\u003c/u\u003e\u003c/strong\u003e\u003cbr\u003e2019 was the year when Salsa God expanded our sales channels by adding Convenience Stores, Meal Kit Companies, Hotels, Airlines and Airports. Our channel expansion increases the number of locations that consumers can find Salsa God products and, as a result, our sales! \u0026nbsp; \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eConvenience Stores -\u0026nbsp;\u003c/em\u003eThere are more than 150,000 convenience stores vs. 40,000 grocery stores in the U.S. The Chips \u0026amp; Salsa Combo Pack is a perfect Grab \u0026amp; Go item that was designed for C-Stores. \u0026nbsp;We are super excited about this opportunity, and we recently met with a buyer from Circle K at the Winter Fancy Food Show. \u0026nbsp;The C-Store Channel is a huge opportunity for Salsa God, and we are going to work tirelessly to get our Combo Pack into convenience stores in all 50 states and Canada!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eHotels/Casinos\u003c/em\u003e - The Combo Pack is also a perfect item for Hotels and Casinos. \u0026nbsp;Whether the Combo Pack is part of the mini-bar program or stocked in the sundry store inside the hotel/casino, we think that the Salsa God Combo Pack will begin to rack up lots of $ales! Hilton is running a test of our product in one of their hotels in Midtown Manhattan!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eAirlines and Airports\u003c/em\u003e - In June of 2019, an airline expressed interest in our Combo Pack for their domestic flight program. \u0026nbsp;The airline asked if we could shrink the size of the Combo Pack from 3oz chips/4oz salsa to 2oz chips/3oz salsa, and we are working on their request! \u0026nbsp;Additionally, Salsa God has engaged a company that specializes in placing food products in airlines. \u0026nbsp;We are optimistic that Salsa God Chips \u0026amp; Salsa Combo Packs will begin to pop up on flights within the U.S. by the end of 2020! \u0026nbsp;We are also targeting the convenience stores located in the airports. \u0026nbsp;On The Go Express has placed a few purchase orders and we expect to see our Combo Packs in EWR Newark and IAH Houston airports soon. \u0026nbsp;A number of other companies have expressed interest including Hudson News! \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cem\u003eCollege Campuses\u003c/em\u003e - I remember how much I ate as a college kid, and I know that our Combo Pack will do super well at college campuses throughout the U.S. and Canada. \u0026nbsp;We have had a number of universities reach out to us to source our combo pack. \u0026nbsp;Even more exciting than that is Barnes \u0026amp; Noble College. \u0026nbsp;We were originally slated for a January 2020 launch into 50-100 Barnes \u0026amp; Noble College stores, but this was pushed back to Summer 2020 (hot weather is better for chips \u0026amp; salsa sales). \u0026nbsp;So we are looking forward to becoming a proud supplier of Chips \u0026amp; Salsa to Barnes \u0026amp; Nobel College, Universities and the entire NCAA!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eAll in all, we are very proud of our accomplishments in 2019. \u0026nbsp;We hope all of our investors are as excited as we are. \u0026nbsp;I have spent all of last week in meetings with buyers, distributors and importers in Santiago, Chile and Lima, Peru. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSantiago, Chile\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eMonday of last week, I spent getting to learn more about Chile as a market. \u0026nbsp;I attended a retail briefing hosted by the USDA Foreign Agricultural Service and went on a retail tour of Santiago to get a first-hand view of the retail landscape for specialty food products in Chile. \u0026nbsp;Mexican Food consumption is growing in Chile - I ate at two great Mexican restaurants - and Salsa God will be well poised to help the growth continue. \u0026nbsp;Last Tuesday and Wednesday, Salsa God exhibited at the Espacio Food \u0026amp; Service Trade Show in the USDA Pavilion in Santiago - Chile's largest and most important food trade show. \u0026nbsp;It was a fantastic show, and Salsa God generated significant interest among buyers and importers!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/011/696/11696-1568756388-288b8fad9092dee93ff18de3b70ad14ced8ec2c7.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\" style=\"width: 477px;\"\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eLima, Peru\u003c/strong\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLima is an amazing city of 10 million people, and it is a city filled with amazing restaurants. \u0026nbsp;Peru has certainly earned its reputation as one of the best culinary spots on the planet. \u0026nbsp;From amazing ceviche to one of the best sandwiches I have ever had in my life at La Lucha Sanguchería Criolla (\u003ca href=\"https://lalucha.com.pe/\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003ehttps://lalucha.com.pe/\u003c/a\u003e), Lima will leave you feeling delighted and full! During my stay in Lima, I met with over 10 buyers, importers and distributors who were interested in bringing Salsa God to Peru. I had two very serious discussions with importers who are very, very eager to become the exclusive distributor of Salsa God in Peru, so we expect to add Peru as Salsa God's 4th market in Q4 2019 or Q1 2020! I can't wait to begin exporting to Peru so I will have an excuse to come visit Lima again and eat copious amounts of their amazing food (and visit Cusco again). \u0026nbsp;\u003cimg src=\"https://uploads.republic.co/p/images/attachments/original/000/011/697/11697-1568756938-bd9cefc01d52cddf0d18acc17dc7101e982227fb.jpg\" class=\"fr-fic fr-dib\"\u003e(Machu Picchu!!!)\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eI'm looking forward to getting back to the U.S. \u0026nbsp;Thank you everyone for your patience as I know my responses to your questions were not as prompt as normal. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThank you to all of the wonderful people that I met in Santiago, Chile and Lima, Peru. \u0026nbsp;It was a name=\"offerings_update[body]\" id=\"offerings_update_form_1111_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eAbout the same time last year, I was on a 17 hour flight from LAX (Los Angeles) to MEL (Melbourne, Australia) with the hopes of selling Salsa God Salsa in a place I called home for nearly six months. \u0026nbsp;And just last year, during that week spent in Australia and New Zealand, Salsa God secured a distributor to begin exporting our delicious products to Australia. \u0026nbsp;Earlier this year, I spent a week in Toronto and Montreal hoping to land our 2nd export market. \u0026nbsp;While I didn't get a \"yes\" during my trip to Canada, after requesting a follow up meeting with a buyer I met with in Toronto, Salsa God did get that yes. \u0026nbsp;Preparations are now underway to make our launch go amazingly in our friendly neighbor to the north (Canada). \u0026nbsp;In the meantime, an incredible opportunity has arisen to try and secure a 3rd and 4th export market, so I am currently in South America promoting Salsa God's amazing restaurant-style salsas. \u0026nbsp;Throughout next week, I will be meeting with importers, distributors and large retailers in Santiago, Chile and Lima, Peru. \u0026nbsp;This trip was made possible through the federally funded Market Access Program, which is a part of the Foreign Agricultural Services of the USDA. \u0026nbsp; Wish me the best of luck, and please forgive my slow responses to your messages and discussion topics. \u0026nbsp;I am overjoyed by the amount of support (and funding) that Salsa God has received this past week, and I promise to respond to everything in short order. \u0026nbsp;In the meantime, please send me any suggestions for great restaurants/things to do/places to go in Santiago, Chile or Lima, Peru!\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eDanny\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/textarea\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cdiv class='s-marginTop1_5 s-marginPullHoriz1 s-paddingHoriz1 s-borderTop1
